# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 5709 files · ~13,620,043 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 314 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 197, (none) 34, .css 16)

## Summary
- 69886 nodes · 206917 edges · 1455 communities (1320 shown, 135 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8606 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5c689057`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
